# Handoff: original-normal projection flows

JOINT UNVERIFIED. Original target OPEN. One analytical invocation completed;
STOP, no automatic renewal. Parent closes allocation `f3573e14d9155ee2`.

Read `REPORT.md` for the complete assumptions, coefficient conventions,
derivations, and witnesses. No new accepted fact or full-class bound.

## What is usable as a draft

For exact U certificates `J+sum lambda_ij Q_ij` that are globally PSD in
the original physical gradients, one auxiliary `p=P(x_0/M)`, and zero:

1. Set `mu_ij=a_j lambda_ij`, with physical `a_j=1` and `a_p=1/M`.
   Exact cancellation forces all nontrivial star rows to zero and
   `out_i-in_i=2(1_(i=n)-1_(i=p))`.
2. Common-gradient contact forces
   `sum_j mu_ij(t_j-t_i)=2h_i` for `i<n`, and zero at `n,p`, with
   `t_i=1+sum_(k<i)h_k` and `t_p=M=1+2sum h`.
3. For positive total mass, `p` is absorbing and its total incoming mass
   is two. Writing `w_i=mu_(i,p)`, `z=sum_i w_i g_i/2` gives the exact
   decomposition `R=M||p-z||^2+J_z+sum_(physical i,j)mu_ij Q_ij`.
   Hence every such PSD certificate requires
   `J_z=2<x_0,z>-M||z||^2-U_1(x_n)>=0` on every legal physical history.
4. The full Dirichlet identity is REPORT (3.3). Its signed memory term
   remains unpaid. The Markov interpretation supplies no PSD result.

These are statements about a specified sufficient conic certificate
class. No necessity for arbitrary true U inequalities and no dual
attainment theorem was proved.

## The two explicit laws and their first decisive obstructions

- Terminal fan, REPORT (5.1):
  `mu_(n,i)=mu_(i,p)=2h_i/(M-t_i)` for `i<n`, and remaining source flow
  goes `n->p`. All weights are nonnegative and satisfy both interface
  equations. The full Schur diagonal is `w_i(1-M w_i/4)`, which is
  negative for every `h_i>=2`. It cannot handle the H3 large bridge.
- Recursive binary tree, REPORT (6.1)-(6.4):
  parent of physical node `i` in H_m is `i+2^v2(i+1)`; use the clock
  conductances there and canonical absorption weights
  `w_i=2h_i/M`, `w_n=2(1+S)/M`. H1 passes analytically; H2 has the
  complete negative assignment `g_2=v`, all other physical gradients
  zero, `p=A v/M_2`, giving
  `M_2 R=[A^2+2C(1-A)]||v||^2<-17||v||^2/12`.

Both are formal PSD obstructions, not legal U counterexamples.

## Stronger global exclusion and quantitative interface

The canonical absorption pattern itself, independently of **every**
physical multiplier and graph topology, would force the whole-word
primitive energy `V=||x_0||^2-||x_n+Sg_n||^2` to be nonnegative. It is
false already for the actual same-m H3.

Use `C=1+S_2`, `k=beta c`, `S=S_3=2C+k-1`, `C_3=1+S`,
`M=1+2S`, `r=k/C`, and `delta=k(2C+k)-C^2>0`. The interval body
`[-r,1]`, start `C`, has left-block gradients one and right-block
gradients `-r`; whole-block inequalities justify this analytically.
Its primitive energy is
`V=-delta(C^2+C_3 k)/C^2<0`.

For **any** single-p original-row PSD certificate, let
`alpha=sum_(i=0)^3 mu_(i,p)/2`. REPORT (8.9) gives

\[
 \alpha-\frac{C+k}{M}\ge
 \frac{\delta(C^2+C_3k)}{2MSk(C+k)}>0.
\]

Thus changing all physical circulations while keeping canonical
absorption cannot work. A future candidate in this certificate class
must change absorption too, and then prove the complete residual PSD.
The inequality is only necessary; no candidate accomplishing this was
constructed. Additional auxiliary projections, different certificate
classes, and direct `17/16` radius certificates remain outside the
exclusion. These possibilities were not opened as extra mechanisms.

## Evidence and boundary

Accepted inputs: `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` and `931b9653ce0f7201`.
Unaccepted contextual source:
`research/shared_evidence/e5264ef28cade979.md`; no old multiplier array
was frozen. Full original `PROBLEM.md` and current `OPERATOR.md` were
read. The wrong initial `project/shared_evidence` path failed openly
and was corrected to the public research path.

Zero mathematical programs, replay runs, solvers, formal calls,
consults, subagents, installations, truth-store writes, publication,
worker/service control, Goal changes, or lifecycle actions.
No claim of `W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m)`, an all-depth U law, asymptotic
separation, or original Goal completion. Current output is a blocked
route assessment with a precise necessary absorption constraint.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000918 -->
# Original-normal clock flows: coefficient interface and two excluded laws

JOINT UNVERIFIED. Direct analytical invocation, 2026-10-08.
Project `gd-exact-profile-20260926`; allocation `f3573e14d9155ee2`;
resume elaboration `5b362b0a381a2444`.
Original mathematical target: OPEN. No verifier submission or accepted fact.

## 1. Result and scope

This invocation did not obtain an all-depth U inequality or the sufficient
full-class bound `W(H_m) <= 17/(16 D_m)`. It derived the complete coefficient
interface for certificates using all physical original projection normals,
the one auxiliary projection `p=P_D(x_0/M)`, and zero. It then excluded two
explicit laws: a terminal fan and a recursive binary-tree routing.

There is also a topology-independent obstruction, stronger than those two
individual exclusions. For the actual H3, every globally PSD certificate of
this one-auxiliary type must move a strictly positive, explicitly bounded
amount of absorption weight into the left H2-plus-bridge block. In particular,
the usual absorption weights proportional to step mass, with remaining
weight at the terminal node, fail for **every** redistribution among physical
original rows. This does not freeze an old primitive multiplier array.

The precise weight-transfer lower bound is (8.9). It is a necessary
coefficient constraint, not a sufficient recursive interface. The unpaid
global quadratic remains the obstruction to this route. Neither a Markov
interpretation nor the exclusions below establish a bound on the original
minimax problem.

The accepted sources used are:

- `fact_graph/facts/1e0bf3f41457d8e4.md`: finite-word projection-simplex
  universality, original normal orientation, and the actual H3 U law.
- `fact_graph/facts/931b9653ce0f7201.md`: `B_(n_m)/D_m < 47/50` and the
  conditional implication from `W(H_m) <= 17/(16D_m)` to separation.

The public unaccepted source
`research/shared_evidence/e5264ef28cade979.md` was read to identify the two
old join-return mechanisms. Its failed multiplier arrays are not reused as
premises or frozen components here.

## 2. Formal coefficient interface, including zero rows

Fix a finite nonnegative word `h=(h_0,...,h_(n-1))`, and set

\[
 T_i=\sum_{k<i}h_k,\qquad S=T_n,\qquad M=1+2S,\qquad
 t_i=1+T_i,\qquad t_p=M.
\tag{2.1}
\]

On one nonempty compact convex body `D` containing zero, let

\[
 x_i=x_0-\sum_{k<i}h_kg_k,\quad g_i=P_D(x_i),\quad
 p=P_D(x_0/M),\quad
 U_d(x)=\max_{g\in D}\{2\langle x,g\rangle-d\|g\|^2\}.
\]

The physical terminal gradient `g_n` has no associated physical step. The
formal labels are `I={0,...,n,p}` together with a zero label `*`.
Write `G_i=g_i`, `G_p=p`, and

\[
 B_iG=g_i+\sum_{k<i}h_kg_k,\quad B_pG=Mp,\quad
 a_i=1\ (i\le n),\quad a_p=1/M.
\tag{2.2}
\]

Thus `X_j-G_j=a_j(x_0-B_jG)` for nonzero labels, whereas
`X_*=G_*=0`. The original orientation is

\[
 Q_{ij}=\langle X_j-G_j,G_i-G_j\rangle\le0.
\tag{2.3}
\]

We consider only sufficient certificates of the following specific form:
nonnegative constants `lambda_ij`, depending on the word, such that

\[
 R=J+\sum_{i,j}\lambda_{ij}Q_{ij}\quad\hbox{is a globally PSD
 quadratic in all formal coordinates},\qquad
 J=U_M(x_0)-U_1(x_n).
\tag{2.4}
\]

In (2.4), the U values are replaced by their actual-maximizer expressions
before taking the formal quadratic:

\[
 J=2\langle x_0,p-g_n\rangle
   +2\Big\langle g_n,\sum_{k<n}h_kg_k\Big\rangle
   +\|g_n\|^2-M\|p\|^2.
\tag{2.5}
\]

On an actual projection history, (2.4) would give `J>=R>=0`.
No converse is asserted: this report does not prove conic dual attainment
or that every true U inequality has a certificate (2.4).

Rows with `j=*` and diagonal rows are identically zero and may be removed.
For every remaining row define

\[
 \mu_{ij}=a_j\lambda_{ij}.
\tag{2.6}
\]

The coefficient of `x_0` must vanish. Indeed, there is no `||x_0||^2` term;
a nonzero linear coefficient in `x_0` prevents global PSD. Set all nonstar
gradients equal in this coefficient equation. Contributions of nonstar
rows cancel pairwise and the objective coefficient is zero, leaving
`-sum_j mu_(*,j) G`. Consequently

\[
 \mu_{*,j}=0\quad\hbox{for every nonstar }j.
\tag{2.7}
\]

This conclusion uses nonnegativity and exact cancellation, not a presumed
constant-gradient legal history. All nontrivial star rows vanish. On `I`,
the remaining cancellation equations are exactly

\[
 b_i:=\operatorname{out}_i(\mu)-\operatorname{in}_i(\mu)
     =2(\mathbf1_{i=n}-\mathbf1_{i=p}).
\tag{2.8}
\]

Here the edge convention is `i -> j` for `mu_ij`; it follows the indices
of the original row, not the physical GD direction. After (2.8), the entire
remaining polynomial is

\[
 R=2\Big\langle g_n,\sum_{k<n}h_kg_k\Big\rangle+
       \|g_n\|^2-M\|p\|^2
       +\sum_{i,j\in I}\mu_{ij}\langle B_jG,G_j-G_i\rangle.
\tag{2.9}
\]

This verifies the sign in the root lead. Original coefficients are recovered
without ambiguity by `lambda_ij=mu_ij` for physical `j` and
`lambda_(i,p)=M mu_(i,p)`.

For a coefficient-level specification of every matrix entry, let `e_i`
be the coordinate columns, `H=sum_(k<n)h_k e_k`, `b_j` the column of
`B_j`, and `sym(u,v)=(uv^T+vu^T)/2`. Then the symmetric matrix of (2.9) is

\[
 2\operatorname{sym}(e_n,H)+e_ne_n^T-Me_pe_p^T
 +\sum_{i,j}\mu_{ij}\operatorname{sym}(b_j,e_j-e_i).
\tag{2.10}
\]

Every coordinate, including `p` and `g_n`, remains present.

## 3. Constant-gradient contact and absorption

At a formal assignment `G_i=v` for all `i in I`, (2.9) is zero because
`2S+1=M`. A globally PSD quadratic that is zero on this subspace must have
zero first variation there. This follows by substituting `G+u Z` and
allowing both signs of the scalar `u`.

Define the clock drift

\[
 d_i=\sum_j\mu_{ij}(t_j-t_i).
\]

The row term's first variation at coordinate `i` is
`-b_i t_i-d_i`. The first three terms of (2.9) contribute `2h_i`
for `i<n`, `2(S+1)` for `i=n`, and `-2M` for `i=p`.
Using (2.8), all contact equations are therefore

\[
 \boxed{d_i=2h_i\ (i<n),\qquad d_n=d_p=0.}
\tag{3.1}
\]

For `S>0`, `t_p=M>1+S>=t_i` for every physical node. Nonnegative weights
and `d_p=0` force

\[
 \mu_{p,j}=0\ (j\ne p),\qquad
 w_i:=\mu_{i,p}\ge0,\qquad \sum_{i=0}^n w_i=2.
\tag{3.2}
\]

Thus the maximal clock really is absorbing for every certificate in this
class. It was not imposed in advance. Equal physical clocks caused by zero
steps do not affect this argument. At `S=0`, strict clock maximality fails;
no absorption claim is made. The all-zero word's actual U inequality is
trivial because every physical point and the auxiliary input coincide.

For strictly positive words, (2.8) and (3.1) admit a literal absorbing
Markov interpretation. Give each physical node transition probabilities
`P_ij=mu_ij/out_i` and make `p` absorbing. Every preterminal node has
positive outgoing mass by its positive clock drift; `n` has positive
outgoing mass by its divergence two. No closed communicating class can
avoid `p`: a maximal-clock preterminal member would have nonpositive drift,
and if the maximal member is `n`, zero drift would force no edge to the
strictly earlier clocks, contradicting its outgoing mass. Hence absorption
occurs with probability one. Half the edge flow is the expected edge
occupation of this chain started at `n`, by the finite transient balance
equations. This interpretation alone gives no sign to (2.9).

To expose the unpaid term, put

\[
 F_j=\sum_{k<j}h_k(G_k-G_j)\quad(j\le n),\qquad F_p=0.
\]

Then `B_jG=t_jG_j+F_j`. The complete Dirichlet identity is

\[
 \boxed{R=\frac12\sum_{i,j}\mu_{ij}t_j\|G_i-G_j\|^2
 -\sum_{i<n}h_i\|g_i-g_n\|^2
 +\sum_{i,j}\mu_{ij}\langle F_j,G_j-G_i\rangle.}
\tag{3.3}
\]

For its derivation, expand each `t_j<G_j,G_j-G_i>` as a squared difference
plus a norm difference. The norm coefficient is
`-(b_i t_i+d_i)/2`; substitution of (2.8) and (3.1) gives (3.3).
The final term has no assigned sign. Replacing its sum by an expectation
does not pay it.

## 4. Exact absorption-mixture reduction

For any feasible interface (2.8), (3.1), define the fixed convex mixture

\[
 z=\frac12\sum_{i=0}^n w_i g_i.
\tag{4.1}
\]

Here “fixed” refers to the weights; `z` itself depends on the gradients.
Absorption (3.2) gives an exact square completion:

\[
 R=M\|p-z\|^2+K,
\quad
 K=J_z+\sum_{i,j\le n}\mu_{ij}Q_{ij},
\quad
 J_z=2\langle x_0,z\rangle-M\|z\|^2-U_1(x_n).
\tag{4.2}
\]

The `x_0` coefficient of `K` vanishes by (2.8); it is a quadratic only
in physical gradients. Thus (2.4) is PSD if and only if `K` is PSD,
because `p` is an unrestricted formal coordinate and can be set to `z`.
In particular every such certificate necessarily satisfies

\[
 J_z\ge0\quad\hbox{on every actual physical projection history.}
\tag{4.3}
\]

This is a precise restriction on these certificates: they must already
prove the U lower evaluation at one word-dependent convex combination of
the physical gradients. The auxiliary projection supplies the nonnegative
maximization remainder. No actual history is required to have `p=z`.

## 5. Law 1: explicit terminal fan

For every word with `S>0`, prescribe

\[
 w_i=\frac{2h_i}{M-t_i}\ (i<n),\quad
 w_n=2-\sum_{i<n}w_i,
\quad
 \mu_{n,i}=\mu_{i,p}=w_i\ (i<n),\quad
 \mu_{n,p}=w_n.
\tag{5.1}
\]

All unlisted entries are zero. Since `M-t_i=2S-T_i>S` whenever `h_i>0`,
`sum_(i<n) w_i<2`; hence every entry is nonnegative, including `w_n`.
Equations (2.8) hold directly. For `i<n`, clock drift is
`w_i(M-t_i)=2h_i`; at `n` it is
`2S-sum_(i<n)w_i(M-t_i)=0`. This verifies the full contact interface.

Contact makes (2.9) invariant under a common translation of all formal
gradients. Set `g_n=0`. The whole polynomial, including `p`, is

\[
 R=M\Big\|p-\frac12\sum_{i<n}w_i g_i\Big\|^2
 +\sum_{i<n}w_i\|g_i\|^2
 +\sum_{t<i<n}w_i h_t\langle g_t,g_i\rangle
 -\frac M4\Big\|\sum_{i<n}w_i g_i\Big\|^2.
\tag{5.2}
\]

For one fixed `i<n`, set `g_i=v`, every other physical gradient zero,
and `p=w_i v/2`. The entire value is

\[
 R=w_i\left(1-\frac{M w_i}{4}\right)\|v\|^2.
\tag{5.3}
\]

Thus a positive `h_i` requires

\[
 h_i\le 2\left(1-\frac{t_i}{M}\right)<2
\tag{5.4}
\]

for PSD of this law. Every `h_i>=2` violates (5.4). The H3 bridge
`1+k` has `k=beta c>1`, so this law is excluded for H3 and for every
larger hierarchy containing that bridge. This is a negative formal
quadratic assignment, not a legal U counterexample.

The one-step sign calibration is exact: `w_0=w_1=1`, and (5.2) is
`M||p-g_0/2||^2+(1-M/4)||g_0||^2` after `g_1=0`. Its threshold is
`h_0<=3/2`. No numerical evaluation was used.

## 6. Law 2: recursive binary-tree routing

This is the sole recursive family tried after Law 1. It is constructed
from clocks and subtree masses, without an inherited primitive multiplier
array.

First consider any rooted tree on physical nodes `0,...,n`, rooted at
`n`, in which every parent `j=pi(i)` has `j>i`. For a strictly positive
word, let `A_i` be the sum of the step weights `h_v` over the subtree
rooted at `i` (the root `n` carries no step weight). Put

\[
 L_i=t_{\pi(i)}-t_i>0,
\quad
 \mu_{i,\pi(i)}=\frac{2A_i t_i}{M L_i},\qquad
 \mu_{\pi(i),i}=\frac{2A_i t_{\pi(i)}}{M L_i}.
\tag{6.1}
\]

The absorption edges are fixed as

\[
 w_i=\frac{2h_i}{M}\ (i<n),\qquad
 w_n=\frac{2(1+S)}{M}.
\tag{6.2}
\]

All unlisted entries are zero. Every coefficient is nonnegative. Across
a subtree edge the net outward physical flow is `-2A_i/M`; summing at a
node gives physical divergence `-2h_i/M`, and at the root `2S/M`.
Adding (6.2) proves (2.8).

For `Lambda_ij=M mu_ij` on physical edges, the weighted conductance
`nu_ij=Lambda_ij t_j` is symmetric. Therefore
`sum_j Lambda_ij t_j=t_i in_i(Lambda)`. Its clock drift is
`-t_i div_i(Lambda)`: this is `2h_i t_i` at preterminal nodes and
`-2S(1+S)` at `n`. Adding the absorption-edge drift proves (3.1).

For the literal H_m, the explicit recursive tree is

\[
 \ell(i)=v_2(i+1),\qquad
 \pi(i)=i+2^{\ell(i)},\qquad 0\le i<2^m-1,
\tag{6.3}
\]

with root `n=2^m-1`. Each subtree consists of the contiguous copy
`(H_(ell(i)),1+a c^(ell(i)))` ending at `i`. Consequently

\[
 A_i=S_{\ell(i)}+1+a c^{\ell(i)},\qquad L_i=A_i,
\tag{6.4}
\]

and (6.1) simplifies to `mu_(i,pi(i))=2t_i/M` and
`mu_(pi(i),i)=2t_(pi(i))/M`. This specifies every original coefficient
at every depth; both orientations and all absorption edges are retained.

The family has an exact first obstruction already at H2. Write

\[
 A=1+a,\quad B=1+\beta,\quad C=1+2A+B,\quad M_2=1+2(2A+B).
\]

The physical tree is `0--1--3` and `2--3`. Set the full formal assignment

\[
 g_0=g_1=g_3=0,\qquad g_2=v,\qquad p=(A/M_2)v.
\tag{6.5}
\]

The mixture square in (4.2) vanishes. Only the two rows on `2--3`
contribute a nonzero physical diagonal. Substitution in (2.9), including
the absorption square completion, gives the entire value

\[
 M_2R=\big[A^2+2C(1-A)\big]\|v\|^2<0.
\tag{6.6}
\]

For the strict sign, the same-enclosing parameters give
`1/3<a<sqrt(2)-1<1/2` and `B>19/10`. Hence `4/3<A<3/2`,
`C>11/2`, and

\[
 A^2-2C(A-1)<9/4-11/3=-17/12<0.
\]

At H1, the same tree law reduces instead to
`M_1 R=||M_1p-A g_0-(1+A)g_1||^2+(2-A^2)||g_0-g_1||^2`,
which is PSD because `A<sqrt(2)`. Thus H2 is the first exact obstruction
to this explicit recursive law. Assignment (6.5) is not asserted to be a
legal projection history. In particular it does not contradict the
accepted H2 or H3 inequalities.

## 7. A global obstruction to the entire absorption pattern of Law 2

The next exclusion is not a third candidate law. It allows **arbitrary**
nonnegative coefficients on every physical original row while retaining
only the absorption pattern (6.2). It therefore rules out repairing Law 2
by any topology change, extra physical circulation, or global physical
row redistribution that preserves that pattern.

For (6.2),

\[
 z=\frac{\sum_{i<n}h_i g_i+(1+S)g_n}{M}.
\tag{7.1}
\]

Let the primitive energy of the whole word be

\[
 V=\|x_0\|^2-\|x_n+Sg_n\|^2.
\tag{7.2}
\]

Writing `A_vec=sum_(i<n)h_i(g_i-g_n)` gives
`V=2<x_0,A_vec>-||A_vec||^2`. Direct substitution in (4.2) yields

\[
 J_z=V/M,\qquad
 R=M\|p-z\|^2+
   \frac1M\left[V+\sum_{i,j\le n}\Lambda_{ij}Q_{ij}\right],
\quad \Lambda_{ij}=M\mu_{ij}\ge0.
\tag{7.3}
\]

Consequently a PSD certificate with (6.2) would imply `V>=0` on every
actual physical projection history, independently of the physical array.
This is stronger than the U inequality and is false for the actual H3.

To give an analytical H3 sign calibration, use one enclosing `m>=1536`
and put

\[
 C=1+S_2=4+2a+\beta,\quad k=\beta c,\quad
 S=S_3=2C+k-1,\quad C_3=1+S=2C+k,\quad M=1+2S,
\quad r=k/C.
\tag{7.4}
\]

The strict defect is

\[
 \delta=k(2C+k)-C^2>0.
\tag{7.5}
\]

Here is a direct parameter check, not an imported unaccepted claim.
Let `u=sqrt(2)-1=1/lambda`, `lambda=1+sqrt(2)`. Then

\[
 C=\lambda^2-\epsilon\left[1+\frac{4u}{1+\epsilon}\right],
 \quad k=\lambda(1-\epsilon^2),
\]

so

\[
 k-uC=\epsilon\left[u+\frac{4u^2}{1+\epsilon}-\lambda\epsilon\right]>0.
\]

The last bracket is positive already from `u>2/5`, `lambda<5/2`, and
`epsilon<1/10`. Finally
`delta=(k-uC)(k+lambda C)>0`.

Consider the interval body `D=[-r,1]` and start `x_0=C`. The entire
first H2 and its bridge query have gradient one: every prefix mass of H2
is at most `C-1`, so its query is at least one. The bridge `1+k` puts
the next query at `-k`. The whole right H2 has gradient `-r`, since for
each of its prefix masses `T<=C-1`,
`-k+rT<=-k+r(C-1)=-r`. Its terminal is `x_7=g_7=-r`.
These whole-block inequalities establish the original physical samples;
no trajectory, arithmetic, or recurrence program was run.

On these samples (7.2) is

\[
 V=C^2-C_3^2r^2
   =-\frac{\delta(C^2+C_3k)}{C^2}<0.
\tag{7.6}
\]

For **every** physical array with `Lambda_ij>=0`, set the formal `p` to
(7.1). Its entire polynomial (7.3) is at most `V/M<0`, because all actual
physical `Q_ij<=0`. This gives one uniform full-space obstruction to the
whole absorption pattern (6.2), rather than to a fixed multiplier table.

The formal auxiliary value used for this PSD test need not be
`P_D(x_0/M)`. Therefore (7.6) is a primitive-energy counterexample and a
certificate obstruction, not a counterexample to the H3 U law. The latter
is an accepted fact and remains intact.

## 8. Quantitative absorption transfer forced at H3

Keep the H3 data (7.4). For an arbitrary certificate of the full original-row
class (2.4), define the left-block absorption mass

\[
 \alpha=\frac12\sum_{i=0}^3\mu_{i,p},\qquad 0\le\alpha\le1.
\tag{8.1}
\]

On the two constant-gradient blocks above, its mixture is exactly

\[
 z=(1+r)\alpha-r.
\tag{8.2}
\]

Since `U_1(x_7)=r^2`, the topology-independent necessary condition (4.3)
becomes

\[
 2Cz-Mz^2-r^2\ge0.
\tag{8.3}
\]

The canonical left mass and mixture from (6.2) are

\[
 \alpha_0=\frac{C+k}{M},\qquad z_0=\frac{C-Sr}{M},\qquad
 2Cz_0-Mz_0^2-r^2=V/M<0.
\tag{8.4}
\]

Set `d=z-z_0=(1+r)(alpha-alpha_0)`. Subtraction gives the exact scalar
condition

\[
 0\le\frac VM+2Sr\,d-Md^2.
\tag{8.5}
\]

Because `V<0` and `Sr>0`, (8.5) forces `d>0`. Dropping only the
nonpositive final square gives the explicit necessary lower bound

\[
 d\ge\frac{-V}{2MSr}.
\tag{8.6}
\]

One can retain the square for a sharp interval. The discriminant is
positive: `C>5`, `k<C/2`, and `M=4C+2k-1<5C` imply
`Mr^2<5C/4<C^2`. Thus (8.3) is exactly

\[
 \frac{C-\sqrt{C^2-Mr^2}}{M}\le z\le
 \frac{C+\sqrt{C^2-Mr^2}}{M}.
\tag{8.7}
\]

In particular the sharp required shift above canonical is

\[
 \alpha-\alpha_0\ge
 \frac{Sr-\sqrt{C^2-Mr^2}}{M(1+r)}>0.
\tag{8.8}
\]

The square-root-free lower bound (8.6), using (7.6), is

\[
 \boxed{\alpha-\frac{C+k}{M}\ge
 \frac{\delta(C^2+C_3k)}{2MSk(C+k)}>0.}
\tag{8.9}
\]

This constraint holds for every certificate in the specified one-auxiliary
full original-row PSD class, allowing all physical pairs and all
nonnegative weights. It identifies a quantitatively necessary change in
the absorption distribution. It does not control the remaining matrix
after making that change, and no third law is proposed or tried here.

## 9. Original target, remaining gap, and resource closeout

If an all-depth exact U inequality were established, the selected-minimizer
bound `U_M(x_0)<=||x_0-x_star||^2/M` and accepted finite-word universality
would give the corresponding full-class `W(H_m)<=1/D_m`. The weaker
full-class target `17/(16D_m)` would already suffice by accepted
`931b9653ce0f7201`. This invocation proves neither bound.

The exclusions concern exact-U certificates (2.4). They do not exclude
other auxiliary samples, certificates outside this cone, a different
absorption distribution, or a direct relaxed radius certificate. In
particular, the small positive transfer in (8.9) is not an all-depth
estimate and does not settle the `17/16` allowance. Closing the signed
memory term in (3.3), with a complete global PSD estimate at every depth,
remains a substantive unresolved requirement.

Exactly two laws were considered: (5.1) and the recursive routing
(6.1)-(6.4). Sections 7-8 analyze a necessary condition on the second
law's absorption pattern and then on the common certificate class; they
do not introduce a third candidate. No extra model, agent, consult,
mathematical program, symbolic tool, arithmetic/trajectory/recurrence
replay, solver, optimizer, finite-depth scan, or formal submission was
used. All new derivations were analytical. Only public text reads,
Markdown persistence, and byte-integrity operations were used.

The initial source read used `project/shared_evidence/...` and returned
`Cannot find path`. A bounded public filename search and the parent's
clarification located `project/research/shared_evidence/...`; that actual
file was read. No private worker memory or logs were accessed. Early
combined output was truncated; the decisive accepted Part B statements
and the old source's two exclusion sections were reread in bounded chunks.

The application Goal remains paused; current-turn authorization was the
operator's explicit continuation. No Goal, worker, service, truth store,
finalization, publication, or lifecycle action was taken. Parent closes
the allocation. This invocation returns STOP without automatic renewal.

## One mechanism, at most two explicit circulation laws

Explore conservative nonnegative flows on ALL ORIGINAL projection normals and a Markov/Dirichlet-energy interpretation of constant-gradient clock contact. Do not freeze the old primitive multiplier array or recycle a failed two-row/rank-two join repair.

Independently derive the full coefficient interface. For wordh lengthn,S=sumh,M=1+2S,g_i=P_D(x_i),x_i=x0-sum_(t<i)h_tg_t,p=P_D(x0/M), use auxiliary nodep and zero nodestar. Original normals Q_ij=<X_j-G_j,G_i-G_j><=0, with X_i=x_i,G_i=g_i and X_p=x0/M,G_p=p. Target J=U_M(x0)-U1(x_n).

Define mu_ij=a_j lambda_ij with a_j=1 on physical nodes,a_p=1/M. Cancellation in J+sum lambda Q suggests out_i(mu)-in_i(mu)=2(1_(i=n)-1_(i=p)). Star edges may vanish for these full PSD certificates by constant-gradient contact; prove rather than assume.
With t_i=1+sum_(k<i)h_k,t_p=M, contact suggests sum_j mu_ij(t_j-t_i)=2h_i fori<n and0 fori=n,p. Maximal clock may force p absorbing.

R=2<g_n,sumh_tg_t>+||g_n||^2-M||p||^2+sum_ij mu_ij<B_jG,G_j-G_i>.
Verify all signs and boundary cases. Do not assert every analytic U inequality has this conic certificate without proving dual attainment.

Then try ONE recursive circulation family, at most TWO explicitly distinguished laws, to pay the full signed memory term. A useful deliverable is an all-depth nonnegative-weights/PSD estimate strong enough for full W, or a genuinely new quantitative bridge closing an identified interface. Relabeling an unpaid quadratic as an expectation is BLOCKED, not progress.
One-step and accepted H3 may serve as analytical sign calibrations, with no numerical finite-depth scan. Do not spend the whole call restating an equivalent SDP. Keep exact U propagation and the sufficient relaxed17/16 full-W target in view. At most one substantive relaxation after the first law. Save any complete candidate with every coefficient/assumption for subsequent independent audit.

# Handoff: complete child matrix and joint-reserve gluing

JOINT UNVERIFIED. Grant `35a84cceaf9468c4`. Original target OPEN.
One native invocation and its one optional mathematical process completed.
STOP; no automatic renewal. No new accepted fact or full-class upper bound.

## Extraction result and evidence boundary

The precharged 20-second extraction completed in
`0.7713159790000645` seconds, exit 0, no timeout. No optimizer or conic solve
ran. All imports, numerical loads, Schur complements, solves and exports
were inside that one process. No post-close array loading or mathematical
program occurred. `NUMERICAL_CLOSED.json` is the numerical authority.

On `(A/S,B)`, the raw numerical joint reserve is approximately

- H3: `[[3.14764,-4.35589],[-4.35589,6.30834]]`;
- H4: `[[3.41216,-4.61415],[-4.61415,6.42011]]`.

Raw p diagonals differ slightly from nominal M and were not forced equal.
The complete raw K matrices, all signed original diagnostics and tables,
every computed spectrum/condition number, and the two prescribed block
projection residuals are saved in `raw/extraction.json` and
`raw/diagnostics.txt`. The H4 left/right scalar-projection residual norms
are `0.43036161843568643` and `0.2944709569932239`.
No small singular value was labelled an exact zero or rank.

These remain numerical candidates. They are not exact child certificates
or all-depth induction hypotheses.

## Exact analytical interface retained

REPORT Sections 3-4 state the exact child assumptions and preserve the full
child matrix. With exact SPD K and independent feature columns F,
`T=(F^T K^-1 F)^-1` gives the joint reserve plus the complete complementary
nonnegative square. Both mixed terms and the rest of K are retained.

For two child copies, bridge b, child mass s, ell=s+b, and parent M,
REPORT (4.2)-(4.8) specify every cross ordered coefficient, derive parent
absorption from divergence, and give

`K_parent=a K_left+c K_right+Psi`,

where Psi contains every inherited prefix, actual right-child input and
full cross-memory quadratic. It is an identity, not a PSD conclusion.

## Two precise failed laws

**Law 1: dense absorption product.**
All cross coefficients are `F_ij=eta pi_i pi_j` and
`E_ji=kappa pi_j pi_i`; the complete child physical arrays are scaled by
arbitrary nonnegative a,c. The original preterminal clock equations force
`h_i/pi_i` to be affine in the local clock, unless they force
`a=c=1, eta=kappa=0`, which contradicts the nonzero parent clock shift.
The saved H3 profile lies in a broad rational neighborhood where the
secant slopes on indices 0,3,6 are respectively greater than 1.8 and
less than 1.5. No exact child profile in that neighborhood can use this
law, regardless of its complete joint reserve. See REPORT (5.3)-(5.7).

**Law 2: dense moment-dual joint-feature coupling.**
For every theta in [0,1], set
`q_i=theta h_i/s+(1-theta)pi_i` for preterminal i, and
`q_t=(1-theta)pi_t`. This represents
`g_t+theta A/s+(1-theta)B`.
The unique destination profile matching original normal moments is
`v_t=q_t`, `v_i=q_i-h_i sum_(j>i)v_j`.
Cross coefficients are `F_ij=eta q_i v_j`, `E_ji=kappa q_j v_i`.

For the actual same-m H3 and every absorption profile satisfying
`pi_5<167/1000`, `pi_6>171/1000`, `pi_7<1/5`, the exact coefficient is

`v_5 < -729/100000`

for **every** theta. The saved H3 candidate is strictly within these
bounds. Any nonzero coupling therefore creates a negative original
cross-child coefficient. No edge was clipped or deleted. See REPORT
(6.9)-(6.11). This is a row-sign obstruction, not a legal U counterexample.

Neither law forces canonical absorption or replaces the child matrix by
the old primitive/mixed-cancelled arrays. Both retain every cross-child
ordered pair in their definitions and all corresponding terms in the
gluing identity. No third law was attempted.

## Remaining scope and next-use restriction

No full all-depth certificate, quantitative induction, exact finite
reconstruction, `17/(16D_m)` bound, asymptotic separation, or minimax result
was obtained. The generic reserve identity alone does not close the gap.
Other profiles or complete cross matrices, changes within child arrays,
and properly rederived relaxed-U certificates remain outside these
exclusions. No further task is authorized by this handoff.

updatedAt 1791453602; that administrative update did not expand this grant.

# Complete child reserves and two dense gluing laws

JOINT UNVERIFIED. New allocation `35a84cceaf9468c4`, one native
`gpt-6-astra` / `ultra` analytical invocation, 2026-10-08.
Original minimax target OPEN. No formal submission or accepted new fact.

## 1. Outcome

The single authorized extraction completed. The raw physical Schur matrices
for the saved H3 and H4 complete-graph candidates were numerically positive
definite. Their joint reserves in `(A/S,B)` have substantial negative mixed
entries; those entries were retained. The candidate H4 child blocks are not
close scalar copies of the saved H3 physical-flow block at the level of the
two prescribed Frobenius projections. These are numerical observations.

The analytical work retained each entire child matrix and derived the exact
parent identity (4.7), with **all** cross-child ordered rows present. Two
concrete dense coefficient laws were then tested:

1. Absorption-product coupling between the two complete child graphs.
   Its original clock equations force `h_i/pi_i` to be affine in the child
   clock. A robust three-coordinate test excludes that condition in a
   neighborhood containing the saved H3 absorption vector.
2. Moment-dual coupling for any nonnegative joint mixture of `A/S` and `B`.
   The unique destination profile matching that joint moment has a negative
   coefficient at child index 5 throughout another explicit neighborhood
   containing the saved H3 vector. In fact that coefficient is less than
   `-729/100000` for every mixture parameter. It cannot be deleted or clipped.

These exclusions are independent of how strong the remaining child PSD
reserve is: Law 1 fails the original linear interface, and Law 2 fails
nonnegativity of an original cross-child row before a PSD estimate can help.
They do not exclude other child absorption vectors, general dense gluing,
additional correction terms inside children, or an all-depth U inequality.

No constructive all-depth recursion was obtained. In particular, the
extraction of a two-feature Schur reserve is not claimed as a quantitative
bridge to the target. The route is BLOCKED at the two specified laws.

## 2. Sources, original target, and numerical boundary

The current OPERATOR, original PROBLEM, and complete new TASK were read in
the current session. The prior projection-flow report supplies the notation
and the one-auxiliary coefficient interface, which is rederived below where
needed. The full closed exact-fixture REPORT, HANDOFF and DERIVATION were
read before this extraction. The dense-history report's excluded
mixed-cancelled monotone A/D family was not reopened.

Accepted `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` gives finite-word projection-simplex universality
and the actual same-m H3 U law. Accepted `931b9653ce0f7201` gives

## 3. Exact child assumptions and the joint reserve

The following analytical setup is conditional on an **exact** child
certificate. The numerical candidates above are not silently substituted
for that hypothesis.

Let a child word have `t` steps `h_0,...,h_(t-1)>0`, mass `s`, capacity
`d=1+2s`, and clocks

\[
 t_i=1+\sum_{k<i}h_k,\qquad 0\le i\le t.
\]

The terminal node `t` has no physical child step. Let `C_ij>=0`, `i!=j`,
be all child physical-flow coefficients, and let
`pi_i=w_i/2>=0`, `sum_i pi_i=1`, be its normalized absorption weights.
Set `z=sum_i pi_i g_i`. In the original orientation

\[
 Q_{ij}=\langle x_j-g_j,g_i-g_j\rangle\le0,
\]

an exact child certificate has

\[
 K=2\langle x_{\rm in},z\rangle-d\|z\|^2-U_1(x_{\rm out})
       +\sum_{i,j\le t}C_{ij}Q_{ij}\succeq0.
\tag{3.1}
\]

Here `K` is independent of the formal `x_in` and invariant under a common
translation of all gradients. Its physical flow identities are

\[
 \operatorname{div}_i C=2\mathbf1_{i=t}-2\pi_i,
\tag{3.2}
\]
\[
 \sum_jC_{ij}(t_j-t_i)=2h_i-2\pi_i(d-t_i),
 \quad h_t:=0.
\tag{3.3}
\]

Equation (3.3) includes the terminal row: its value is `-2pi_t s`.
These are obtained by removing absorption edges from the full divergence
`2(e_t-e_p)` and clock drift `2h_i` at preterminal nodes, zero at `t,p`.

Subtract the terminal gradient and write

\[
 y_i=g_i-g_t\quad(i<t),\qquad
 A=\sum_{i<t}h_i y_i,\qquad B=\sum_{i<t}\pi_i y_i=z-g_t.
\tag{3.4}
\]

If the exact quotient matrix `K_mat` in these coordinates is positive
definite and `F=[h/s,pi_physical]` has two independent columns, define

\[
 T=(F^T K_{\rm mat}^{-1}F)^{-1}.
\tag{3.5}
\]

For each scalar coordinate, with `v=F^T y=(A/s,B)^T`, completion of a
square gives the exact identity

\[
 y^TK_{\rm mat}y
 =v^TTv+
 (y-K_{\rm mat}^{-1}FTv)^TK_{\rm mat}
 (y-K_{\rm mat}^{-1}FTv).
\tag{3.6}
\]

The cross term cancels because `F^T K_mat^{-1}F=T^{-1}`. Summing over
Euclidean coordinates proves the vector-feature identity. Both the joint
mixed term in `v^TTv` and the **entire remaining nonnegative quadratic**
are retained. If the hypotheses of (3.5) fail, this particular inverse
formula is unavailable; no rank assumption or pseudoinverse is inserted.

The saved extraction used the raw p Schur complement in place of an exact
`K_mat`, so its matrices remain numerical diagnostics only.

## 4. Parent identity retaining every physical cross-child row

Concatenate two copies of the child and a central step `b>0`. Let

\[
 \ell=s+b,\qquad S=2s+b,\qquad M=1+2S=d+2\ell.
\]

Let the left terminal/root gradient be `u`, the right terminal be `q`,
and use local indices `0,...,t` on each child. The global right clocks
are `ell+t_i`; the root is the left node `t`, and is a genuine parent
step of size `b`. The right-child input is the **actual** point

\[
 x_R=x_0-\ell u-A_L,
\tag{4.1}
\]

where `A_L=sum h_i(g_i^L-u)`. No child is restarted or reset.

Retain scaled complete child arrays `a C` on the left and `c C` on the
right, with `a,c>=0`. Let every cross-child ordered pair have a coefficient

\[
 F_{ij}=\mu_{i_L,j_R},\qquad
 E_{ji}=\mu_{j_R,i_L},\qquad 0\le i,j\le t.
\tag{4.2}
\]

No cross pair is deleted from the following formulas. Parent absorption
is fixed, rather than guessed, by original divergence:

\[
 w_i^L=2a\pi_i-2a\mathbf1_{i=t}
        -\sum_jF_{ij}+\sum_jE_{ji},
\]
\[
 w_j^R=2c\pi_j+2(1-c)\mathbf1_{j=t}
        +\sum_iF_{ij}-\sum_iE_{ji}.
\tag{4.3}
\]

The total of (4.3) is two, but individual nonnegativity and all clock
equations still have to be checked. For physical destinations, original
`lambda=mu`; for the parent auxiliary `p=P(x_0/M)`,
`lambda_(i,p)=M w_i`. No extra projection or star source is used here.

For any exact-U parent certificate satisfying its clock equations, the
common-gradient direction is null and one may put `q=0` in its quotient.
Use that notation in the next identity. Thus

\[
 z_L=u+B_L,\quad z_R=B_R,\quad
 A_R=\sum_{j<t}h_jg_j^R.
\]

Let the full cross-gradient flux and the parent mixture be

\[
 V_{\times}=\sum_{i,j}F_{ij}(g_i^L-g_j^R)
                  +E_{ji}(g_j^R-g_i^L),
\qquad z_P=aB_L+cB_R-\tfrac12V_{\times}.
\tag{4.4}
\]

This is precisely `sum(w_i g_i)/2` from (4.3), not a replaced auxiliary
gradient. Define the original prefix forms

\[
 D_i^L=g_i^L+\sum_{k<i}h_kg_k^L,
\qquad
 D_j^R=\ell u+A_L+g_j^R+\sum_{k<j}h_kg_k^R,
\]

and retain the complete cross-memory quadratic

\[
 C_{\times}=\sum_{i,j}F_{ij}\langle D_j^R,g_i^L-g_j^R\rangle
              +E_{ji}\langle D_i^L,g_j^R-g_i^L\rangle.
\tag{4.5}
\]

The child expressions before adding their physical normals are

\[
 J_L=2\langle x_0,B_L\rangle+2\langle u,A_L\rangle
       -2d\langle u,B_L\rangle-d\|B_L\|^2,
\]
\[
 J_R=2\langle x_0-\ell u-A_L,B_R\rangle-d\|B_R\|^2.
\tag{4.6}
\]

Since a cross normal equals
`<x_0,g_source-g_destination>-<D_destination,g_source-g_destination>`,
(4.4) cancels its complete `x_0` coefficient. The parent physical Schur
quadratic is exactly

\[
 \boxed{K_P=aK_L+cK_R+\Psi,}
\tag{4.7}
\]
\[
\begin{split}
 \Psi={}&-M\|z_P\|^2-2a\langle u,A_L\rangle
       +2ad\langle u,B_L\rangle+ad\|B_L\|^2\\
 &+2c\ell\langle u,B_R\rangle
       +2c\langle A_L,B_R\rangle+cd\|B_R\|^2-C_{\times}.
\end{split}
\tag{4.8}
\]

This accounts for both inherited prefixes, the actual bridge and right
input, all child rows, and all cross rows. Before quotienting, the parent
remainder also contains the exact square `M||p-z_P||^2`.

If (3.6) is available, substituting it into (4.7) retains
`a v_L^T T v_L+c v_R^T T v_R` and both complete nonnegative child
remainders. It does **not** assign a sign to (4.8). Thus (4.7) is an exact
interface; an unspecified PSD bound on its remaining quadratic would be
an unresolved theorem-level requirement. The next two sections instead
test explicit original coefficient laws before making such a claim.

## 5. Law 1: dense absorption-product coupling

For arbitrary scalars `a,c,eta,kappa>=0`, use the complete inherited child
arrays and set, for every cross-child pair,

\[
 F_{ij}=\eta\pi_i\pi_j,\qquad
 E_{ji}=\kappa\pi_j\pi_i.
\tag{5.1}
\]

All pairs are present in the law; no numerical support threshold is used.
Its full absorption array from (4.3) is

\[
 w_i^L=(2a-\eta+\kappa)\pi_i-2a\mathbf1_{i=t},
\quad
 w_j^R=(2c+\eta-\kappa)\pi_j+2(1-c)\mathbf1_{j=t}.
\tag{5.2}
\]

These weights are not canonical. Even allowing all choices in (5.1),
the clock equations impose a rigid condition independent of the PSD
reserve. Define the probability-weighted local clock
`tau=sum_i pi_i t_i`. At any preterminal left node, the inherited,
cross, and absorption drifts respectively are

\[
 a[2h_i-2\pi_i(d-t_i)],\quad
 \eta\pi_i(\ell+\tau-t_i),\quad
 (2a-\eta+\kappa)\pi_i(M-t_i).
\]

Their sum must equal `2h_i`. Hence

\[
 2(a-1)h_i+\pi_i(L-\kappa t_i)=0,
\quad
 L=-2ad+\eta(\ell+\tau)+(2a-\eta+\kappa)M.
\tag{5.3}
\]

For the matched preterminal right node the same calculation, now keeping
its actual global clock `ell+t_i`, gives

\[
 2(c-1)h_i+\pi_i(R-\eta t_i)=0,
\]
\[
 R=-2cd+\kappa(\tau-\ell)
       +(2c+\eta-\kappa)(M-\ell).
\tag{5.4}
\]

Suppose the positive vector `h_i/pi_i`, `i<t`, is not affine in `t_i`.
Equation (5.3) then forces `a=1`: otherwise it explicitly expresses that
vector as an affine function. With `a=1` and distinct preterminal clocks,
(5.3) forces `kappa=0` and `L=0`. Likewise (5.4) forces `c=1`, `eta=0`.
But these values give

\[
 L=2(M-d)=4\ell>0,
\tag{5.5}
\]

a contradiction. Thus this entire scalar-parameter law is impossible for
every such child, before checking root/terminal clocks or PSD.

### Robust H3 input test

This failure is not based on a near-zero floating determinant. Consider
the following open rational neighborhood of a child absorption profile:

\[
 26.8<h_0/\pi_0<26.9,\quad
 17.9<h_3/\pi_3<18.0,\quad
 8.2<h_6/\pi_6<8.3,
\tag{5.6}
\]

with `t_0=1`, `5.82<t_3<5.84` and `12.65<t_6<12.66`.
The literal saved H3 h, clock and absorption text is inside these boxes.
For every real profile in this neighborhood,

\[
 \frac{h_0/\pi_0-h_3/\pi_3}{t_3-t_0}
 >\frac{8.8}{4.84}>1.8,
\]
\[
 \frac{h_3/\pi_3-h_6/\pi_6}{t_6-t_3}
 <\frac{9.8}{6.81}<1.5.
\tag{5.7}
\]

The two secant slopes cannot coincide. Consequently no exact child
certificate whose coefficients lie in this neighborhood can be extended
by Law 1, irrespective of its complete matrix or its joint reserve.
No assertion that an exact child certificate in this neighborhood has
already been reconstructed is needed or made.

This excludes (5.1), not general cross matrices in (4.2). In particular,
the positive finite complete-graph results do not contradict it.

## 6. Law 2: dense moment-dual coupling of the two joint features

After the first failure, the second law changes the destination profile
rather than dropping cross rows. Its purpose is to place the **linear
cross-memory moment** in the span of the terminal gradient and the actual
joint features `(A/s,B)`. The remaining quadratic terms are retained.

For `0<=theta<=1`, define a probability profile

\[
 q_i=\theta h_i/s+(1-\theta)\pi_i\quad(i<t),\qquad
 q_t=(1-\theta)\pi_t.
\tag{6.1}
\]

It represents exactly

\[
 Z_\theta=\sum_iq_i g_i
     =g_t+\theta A/s+(1-\theta)B.
\tag{6.2}
\]

The physical prefix operator is

\[
 D_j^{\rm local}=g_j+\sum_{k<j}h_kg_k.
\]

Choose its destination weights `v` by the exact moment-matching rule

\[
 \sum_j v_j D_j^{\rm local}=Z_\theta
 \quad\hbox{for all formal child gradients}.
\tag{6.3}
\]

The operator is triangular with diagonal one, so this rule is unique:

\[
 v_t=q_t,\qquad
 v_i=q_i-h_i\sum_{j>i}v_j\quad(i<t).
\tag{6.4}
\]

This is an analytical definition, not a program or an executed recurrence
replay. Define all physical cross-child coefficients by

\[
 F_{ij}=\eta q_i v_j,\qquad
 E_{ji}=\kappa q_j v_i,
\qquad \eta,\kappa\ge0,
\tag{6.5}
\]

retaining arbitrary nonnegative complete-child scales `a,c`. Parent
absorption is again **all** of (4.3). Both orientations and all physical
pairs are specified; negative coefficients, if present, cannot be pruned.

For example, the forward part of (4.5) factors exactly as

\[
 \eta\left\langle
     \Big(\sum_jv_j\Big)(\ell u+A_L)+Z_\theta^R,
     Z_\theta^L\right\rangle
 -\eta\sum_jv_j\langle D_j^R,g_j^R\rangle.
\tag{6.6}
\]

The second term is still a full quadratic; it has not been erased or
declared positive. The reverse part has the analogous exact expansion
with `sum_i v_i D_i^L=Z_theta^L`. Thus the proposed law has a specific
joint-feature motivation and still falls under the complete identity
(4.7). It fails earlier, at original-row nonnegativity.

### An unavoidable negative destination coefficient near the H3 candidate

For H3, there are seven steps and eight physical gradients. Consider all
exact child profiles satisfying the broad inequalities

\[
 \pi_5<\frac{167}{1000},\qquad
 \pi_6>\frac{171}{1000},\qquad
 0\le\pi_7<\frac15.
\tag{6.7}
\]

The saved values are respectively
`0.16631937545300204`, `0.17134412866418472`, and
`0.19729423773748717`; all lie strictly inside (6.7).
For every same-enclosing `m>=1536`, the exact physical word also satisfies

\[
 \frac{199}{100}<h_5<2,\qquad
 1<h_6<\frac{283}{200},\qquad h_6-1>\frac25,
 \qquad s<\frac{27}{2}.
\tag{6.8}
\]

To check the only less immediate lower bound, put
`u=sqrt(2)-1>41/100` and use `epsilon<1/100`:
`a=u(1-epsilon)/(1+epsilon)>(41/100)(99/101)>2/5`.
The upper bound on `h_6=1+a` follows from `h_6<sqrt(2)<283/200`.
Also `h_5=2-epsilon`. Finally
`C=1+S_2<lambda^2<6`, `k=beta c<lambda<5/2`, and
`s=2C+k-1<27/2`.

The last three coefficients of the unique rule (6.4) are explicitly

\[
 v_7=(1-\theta)\pi_7,
\]
\[
 v_6=\theta h_6/s+(1-\theta)(\pi_6-h_6\pi_7),
\]
\[
 v_5=-\theta\frac{h_5(h_6-1)}s
 +(1-\theta)\big[\pi_5-h_5\pi_6+h_5(h_6-1)\pi_7\big].
\tag{6.9}
\]

All terms in this coefficient, including the terminal mass and the signed
memory contribution, are present. By (6.7)-(6.8), the bracket obeys

\[
\begin{split}
 \pi_5-h_5\pi_6+h_5(h_6-1)\pi_7
 &<\frac{167}{1000}
  -\frac{199}{100}\frac{171}{1000}
  +2\frac{83}{200}\frac15\\
 &=-\frac{729}{100000}.
\end{split}
\tag{6.10}
\]

The other negative coefficient satisfies

\[
 \frac{h_5(h_6-1)}s>
 \frac{199}{100}\frac25\frac2{27}
 =\frac{199}{3375}>\frac{729}{100000}.
\]

Therefore, **for every** `theta in [0,1]`,

\[
 \boxed{v_5<-\frac{729}{100000}<0.}
\tag{6.11}
\]

If `eta>0` and any source `q_i>0`, the literal original coefficient
`mu_(i_L,5_R)=eta q_i v_5` is negative. If `kappa>0`, the reverse
coefficient with destination `5_L` is negative. Since `q` is a probability
profile, a positive source always exists. Setting both cross scales to zero
does not glue the children: if `a>0`, (4.3) makes the left terminal
absorption `-2a(1-pi_t)<0`; if `a=0`, every left preterminal node has
zero available drift despite its positive physical step.

Thus Law 2 has no admissible nontrivial original-row realization throughout
this neighborhood. Its failure occurs before choosing a joint-reserve
inequality, changing a child scaling, or testing a parent Schur complement.
The negative entry is not a legal objective-error counterexample.

The scope is exactly the moment-matching rule (6.3) with the nonnegative
joint-feature mixture (6.1). Other moment profiles, extra correction
quadratics, changes to internal child arrays, or general dense cross
matrices are not excluded. No third law was attempted.

## 7. Interpretation and the remaining mathematical gap

The two-feature reserve is well-defined under explicit exact SPD and
independence hypotheses, and its full remainder is preserved in (3.6).
Neither prescribed dense cross-block law reaches a stage where that
reserve can pay the join. Law 1 tries to retain the absorption distribution
on both ends of a cross row and loses the original clock equations. Law 2
matches the cross-memory moment to the joint feature and obtains an
inadmissible destination coefficient. These are different original
coefficient obstructions, not variations of the old fixed primitive array.

The numerical block residuals and singular values suggest that the saved
H4 uses more structure than either factorized law. They do not identify an
exact alternative. No finite fit, generic Schur formula, or hypothetical
reserve recursion has been promoted to progress toward the full target.

An all-depth construction would still have to specify every original
coefficient and absorption weight, prove their signs and all clock equations,
and establish the complete parent PSD estimate with no omitted cross term.
The exact-U version would imply `W(H_m)<=1/D_m` by the accepted polar-minimum
bound and finite-word universality. A direct relaxed construction could aim
at `17/(16D_m)`, but would require a newly derived coefficient interface;
the exact common-gradient null reduction cannot simply be carried over.
Neither construction was obtained here.

# Main independent textual audit of the selected joint theorem

UNVERIFIED audit, not service acceptance. The main agent has completely read the original projection-simplex candidate, its independent native AUDIT, and the full actual H3 report. Frozen source hashes are in SOURCE_MANIFEST.json. No mathematical program or formal call was used for this review.

Part A already survived the separate native audit: strict-source ordered rows, equal-gradient labels, zero gradients and zero steps, moving lift coordinates, strict simplex perturbation, all terminal normals, orthogonal compression and the EXACT polar-cone minimum distance, and the order fixed-word/source/tolerance/regularization/lift/compression were checked. The source proof is included rather than assumed as an unverified predecessor. Its approximation need not be uniform across words because its supremum equality is pointwise in each fixed word.

For Part B the main independent algebra check rederived the load-bearing bridge identity. With left mass T and C=1+T, physical endpoint v and a=P(v), bridge1+k, w=v-(1+k)a, h=C+k, trailing D, and r=P(w/D), put e=a-r and X=v+T*a. Then w+h*r=X-h*e. The two actual nonnegative normals Np=<v-a,a-r> and Nr=<w-D*r,r-a> obey Np+Nr=(D+k)<a,e>-D||e||^2. Thus
 ||X||^2-||w+h*r||^2
 =h[2CD/(D+k)-h]||e||^2
 +2h(1+C/(D+k))Np+2hC/(D+k)Nr.
The square coefficient is exactly h[CD-k(C+D+k)]/(D+k), matching the frozen proof. For the actual H3, both H2 children have the displayed complete eight-row positive primitive certificate; the root has C>5,k<C/2,D>=2C-1, making the coefficient strictly positive. Adding the RIGHT child's exact U identity uses its ACTUAL initial maximizer r=P(w/D), not an independent point. Both H2 copies, every physical leaf, the real terminal, all radial terms and both actual maximization remainders are retained. Their convex-mixture weights sum to the actual denominator, so the projection normals have the stated sign. No primitive positivity of H3 or U-only child closure is assumed.

Part C uses only U_1=2F, the true polar-minimum square completion and Part A's pointwise supremum equality. H3 has seven positive steps, so the required simplex dimensions are at most8. The Huber lower instance retains every original step and the original radius, and its final2f equals1/(1+2S3). No numerical benchmark value, asymptotic passage or arbitrary-height rate is involved.

No blocking gap was identified at this selected scope in this textual audit. A service acceptance remains required before the theorem is a Danus fact. A new gap discovered during canonical preparation must close the selected slot unused, not trigger theorem weakening or a broader proof search. The original minimax Goal is OPEN.
